* Your energy usage is calculated from your gas
consumption using a standard industry formula:
 × 2.83 to convert hundreds of cubic feet to cubic
 metres (units consumed)
 × Volume Correction (for temperature & pressure)
 × Calorific Value (energy in each m3 of gas)
 ÷ 3.6 (convert from joules)
 ≈ Usage (in kWh)
For you:
262.4 × 2.83 × 1.02264 × 39.1† ÷ 3.6 = 8247.4
† Average calorific value shown to one decimal place

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Your estimated annual cost
Agile Octopus is a dynamic tariff, so future
costs will vary based on market prices
£2,411.75 a year for gas
This is an estimate based on your expected
annual energy usage, and your current tariff
rates, charges and discounts, including VAT.
Actual billings will vary depending on your
usage and tariff selection. More information
about your current tariff can be found
overleaf.
Could you pay less?
Remember - it might be worth thinking
about switching your tariff or supplier.
For your electricity (on meter point 1900006060728)
As you’re on a dynamic tariff, your rates
move up and down with changes in the
wholesale energy market. This makes it
difficult to provide a long-term cost
forecast that's truly accurate and useful. If

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

* Your energy usage is calculated from your gas
consumption using a standard industry formula:
 × 2.83 to convert hundreds of cubic feet to cubic
 metres (units consumed)
 × Volume Correction (for temperature & pressure)
 × Calorific Value (energy in each m3 of gas)
 ÷ 3.6 (convert from joules)
 ≈ Usage (in kWh)
For you:
97.0 × 2.83 × 1.02264 × 39.1† ÷ 3.6 = 3049.0
† Average calorific value shown to one decimal place

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Could you pay less?
Remember - it might be worth thinking
about switching your tariff or supplier.
For your electricity (on meter point 1900006060728)
As you’re on a dynamic tariff, your rates
move up and down with changes in the
wholesale energy market. This makes it
difficult to provide a long-term cost
forecast that's truly accurate and useful. If
you'd prefer price certainty, get in touch
with us to discuss tariff options.
For your gas (on meter point 638618202)
Good news, you're already on our cheapest
tariff. We'll let you know if this changes.
You may save a few pounds by switching to
Smart Pay-As-You-Go, or if you are a low
user you may also save on the Low Standing
Charge Trial (subject to minimum
consumption threshold). Contact our team
to see if either of these would suit your
circumstances. The Low Standing Charge

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Your estimated annual cost
Agile Octopus is a dynamic tariff, so future
costs will vary based on market prices
£2,448.20 a year for gas
This is an estimate based on your expected
annual energy usage, and your current tariff
rates, charges and discounts, including VAT.
Actual billings will vary depending on your
usage and tariff selection. More information
about your current tariff can be found
overleaf.
Could you pay less?
Remember - it might be worth thinking
about switching your tariff or supplier.
For your electricity (on meter point 1900006060728)
As you’re on a dynamic tariff, your rates
move up and down with changes in the
wholesale energy market. This makes it
difficult to provide a long-term cost
forecast that's truly accurate and useful. If
you'd prefer price certainty, get in touch
with us to discuss tariff options.
You may save a few pounds by switching to
Smart Pay-As-You-Go. Contact our team to
see if this would suit your circumstances.
For your gas (on meter point 638618202)
Good news, you're already on our cheapest
tariff. We'll let you know if this changes.
You may save a few pounds by switching to
Smart Pay-As-You-Go. Contact our team to
see if this would suit your circumstances.

* Your energy usage is calculated from your gas
consumption using a standard industry formula:
 × 2.83 to convert hundreds of cubic feet to cubic
 metres (units consumed)
 × Volume Correction (for temperature & pressure)
 × Calorific Value (energy in each m3 of gas)
 ÷ 3.6 (convert from joules)
 ≈ Usage (in kWh)
For you:
18.0 × 2.83 × 1.02264 × 39.1† ÷ 3.6 = 565.8
† Average calorific value shown to one decimal place

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.

Could you pay less?
Remember - it might be worth thinking
about switching your tariff or supplier.
For your electricity (on meter point 1900006060728)
As you’re on a dynamic tariff, your rates
move up and down with changes in the
wholesale energy market. This makes it
difficult to provide a long-term cost
forecast that's truly accurate and useful. If
you'd prefer price certainty, get in touch
with us to discuss tariff options.
You may save a few pounds by switching to
Smart Pay-As-You-Go. Contact our team to
see if this would suit your circumstances.

* Your energy usage is calculated from your gas
consumption using a standard industry formula:
 × 2.83 to convert hundreds of cubic feet to cubic
 metres (units consumed)
 × Volume Correction (for temperature & pressure)
 × Calorific Value (energy in each m3 of gas)
 ÷ 3.6 (convert from joules)
 ≈ Usage (in kWh)
For you:
30.0 × 2.83 × 1.02264 × 39.2† ÷ 3.6 = 945.4
† Average calorific value shown to one decimal place

Advice and complaints
Contact Citizens Advice if you need help with an
energy problem - for example with your bills or
meters, or if you're struggling to pay for the energy
you use. They're the official source of free and
independent energy advice and support.
Go to: citizensadvice.org.uk/energy or call their
consumer service on 0808 223 1133 Mon to Fri,
9am-5pm
Or, if you live in Scotland, you can contact
energyadvice.scot for independent help.
Go to: energyadvice.scot/email-us, or call their
customer service on 0808 196 8660 Monday to
Friday, 9am to 5pm.
If you feel that our service has not met your
expectations, please get in touch so we can put
things right:
First: Contact our team. If an advisor is not able to
resolve your query, you can ask for it to be escalated
to a specialist or team leader.
Then: If you're still not happy you can ask for an
independent review by an Operations Manager and
we’ll reply within 5 working days.
Finally: If you followed these steps but your
complaint remains unresolved after 8 weeks you can
contact the Energy Ombudsman:
energyombudsman.org or call 0330 440 1624. This is
a free independent service whose decision we must
abide by.
You can read our complaints policy on our website.
